Playing it as a group would leave the puzzle unsolvable, because its words actually belong to different real groups. If a trap exists, identify its 4 words. A trap never takes 3 of its words from a single real group. SCORING This is a One-Shot Puzzle. You get to submit your response exactly once with no feedback. HOW TO PLAY 1. You are given 16 words that form 4 hidden groups of 4 related words each. 2. Sort all 16 words into the 4 groups they belong to. 3. Every word must be used exactly once. Playing it as a group would leave the puzzle unsolvable, because its words actually belong to different real groups. If a trap exists, identify its 4 words. A trap never takes 3 of its words from a single real group. SCORING This is a One-Shot Puzzle. You get to submit your response exactly once with no feedback.
